# Current Full-Product Completion Status

Status date: 2026-07-07.

Romeo is not full-product complete yet. The durable domain repository fragments are now wired into the app runtime when `REPOSITORY_DRIVER=postgres`, the Phase 19 greenfield baseline is locked in [Romeo Greenfield Baseline Lock](./baseline-lock.md), Compose now has a one-shot migration service before app startup plus explicit worker/operations profiles, and the Helm chart now renders the core app/migration/worker/backup surface for external Postgres and CloudNativePG connection-secret modes with schema-validated values plus HPA support. The app runtime now exposes `POSTGRES_POOL_MAX` for database pool sizing plus model-provider timeout, retry, circuit-breaker, fallback, and kill-switch controls, and [Romeo Deployment Sizing](../deployment-sizing.md) defines initial connection-budget math and tier targets. Phase 24 now has delegated OAuth API support for GitHub provider posture, PKCE authorization start, public callback completion, encrypted token persistence, sanitized connection list/revoke APIs, metadata-only admin delegated OAuth posture through `GET /api/v1/admin/delegated-oauth/posture`, audit events, GitHub provider-side grant revocation, server-side callback replay rejection, GitHub connector `delegatedOAuthConnectionId` credential use, repository-backed token refresh serialization with provider-returned refresh-token rotation, revoked/reauthorization fail-closed behavior, OpenAPI, TypeScript SDK, generated Python SDK, Compose, and Helm coverage; UI polish remains open. Phase 34 now has synthetic scale fixture generation, dry-run load-driver evidence, live local Compose scale-smoke evidence for chat writes, knowledge source writes, run writes, attachment writes, comment/notification writes, knowledge queries, admin listing, local-import connector create/sync/readback, and imported OpenAPI tool import/enable/network-policy/operation-enable/preview/dispatch-request enqueue/cancel, a guarded Kubernetes selected-tier load/soak harness, sanitized live Postgres query-plan review evidence for 21 high-volume paths, metadata-only Postgres operational posture through `GET /api/v1/admin/postgres/operational-posture` and `client.admin.postgresOperationalPosture()`, strict optional Postgres operations GA/preflight gating through `pnpm ga:checklist -- --require-postgres-operations-live`, live local Compose object-store outage evidence for attachment read/write failure and recovery, provider failure/idle-timeout runtime redaction tests, pre-output provider retry/circuit-breaker/fallback/kill-switch smoke evidence, metadata-only provider operational summary API/CLI/SDK/UI coverage for circuit, fallback, kill-switch, model-count, and alert-code visibility, metadata-only background job lag/dead-letter summary API/CLI/SDK coverage plus job-lag smoke evidence, a validated Prometheus-compatible operational exporter/rules contract for provider and job signals, a kube-state-metrics-backed Postgres backup failure alert rule, a live alert-firing evidence harness, and loopback alert-firing contract smoke for the Prometheus/Alertmanager readback path; full external worker network execution remains target-deployment evidence, and representative-volume query-plan/slow-query/lock/archival evidence still requires target execution and review. The Postgres repository conformance harness runs against migrated isolated pgvector databases and exercises the admin readiness API with seeded login disabled. Target-quality evidence, Kubernetes NetworkPolicy/CNI enforcement, Kubernetes tiered-RAG isolation, OpenAPI route coverage, enterprise identity contracts, backend capability contracts, and data-rights retention contracts are now explicit GA gates alongside the app, worker, DR, release, edge, alert, and load/soak live evidence gates.

Current GA checklist status is 19 of 29 gates satisfied. The ten environment-required blockers are target-quality evidence capture, Kubernetes live smoke, Kubernetes worker CronJob/crash recovery, Kubernetes NetworkPolicy/CNI enforcement, Kubernetes CloudNativePG/external-Postgres DR, credentialed release readback, Kubernetes tiered-RAG isolation smoke, live edge/WAF/API-gateway enforcement, live alert firing, and Kubernetes load/soak.

Latest full-product evidence hardening makes the enterprise-complete claim explicit without migration churn. `pnpm ga:checklist -- --profile full-product-enterprise --strict` now enables every opt-in enterprise/live evidence gate together, including Qdrant live/DR, hosted CI governance, KEDA, browser automation, target identity, data connector live worker/CNI, tool-dispatch live worker/CNI, production voice, production notifications, analytics authorization, target-quality vector comparison, data-rights retention, billing operations, audit integrity, tenant purge, support bundle, target resilience drills, and target Postgres operations. The default GA checklist remains useful for narrower promotion readiness, but this full-product profile is the completion bar for the PRD/backlog objective.

Latest operator documentation evidence hardening closes the local Phase 35 command-classification gap without migration churn. `pnpm docs:check-commands` now classifies every documented shell fragment as a checked pnpm/script/CLI/deployment command, an environment-specific operator command, or a known local shell utility, and fails on any unclassified executable while returning only aggregate category counts and sanitized executable names for failures. `romeo.docs-command-check.v1`, `pnpm ga:checklist`, `pnpm ga:bundle`, and `pnpm smoke:ga:evidence-contract` now require `documented_commands_classified`, `commandPosture.everyCommandClassified=true`, zero unclassified commands, and explicit proof that raw command text is not returned. This strengthens the "tested or marked environment-specific" requirement for operator docs; clean-environment execution of selected Compose, Kubernetes, CloudNativePG, release, air-gap, upgrade, rollback, and troubleshooting paths remains target/operator evidence.

Latest live target evidence hardening closes a stale-artifact acceptance gap without schema or migration churn. `pnpm ga:checklist` now rejects missing, future-dated, or stale `generatedAt` timestamps for target-quality, Kubernetes live/worker/NetworkPolicy/DR/tiered-RAG/load-soak, live edge enforcement, live alert firing, and optional Qdrant live/DR evidence. Kubernetes load/soak evidence must also identify the namespace, release, service, and deployment it exercised. `pnpm smoke:ga:evidence-contract` now proves timestamp-less and stale target evidence fails instead of allowing placeholder `status=passed` artifacts. Live collection in the selected target environment is still required for the same ten GA blockers.

Latest Kubernetes live-smoke bootstrap hardening closes a local disposable-cluster execution gap without weakening GA image provenance. When `pnpm smoke:kubernetes:live` builds the app image locally and the active context is a `kind-*` cluster, the harness now loads that image into the kind cluster before Helm install and records metadata-only `localImageLoadedToKind` posture. Target GA evidence still requires a reviewed registry-hosted digest-pinned app image plus digest-pinned dependency images; kind image loading is only for local/staging evidence bootstrap.

Latest Kubernetes restricted-pod runtime hardening fixes a live migration-job failure found in a disposable kind run. Helm now sets writable `/tmp`-based `HOME`, `XDG_CACHE_HOME`, `COREPACK_HOME`, and `PNPM_HOME` env vars for app, migration, backup, and worker containers, and the live-smoke seed Job gets the same env. `pnpm smoke:kubernetes:render` now asserts `writable_node_cache_env_render` so restricted non-root pods cannot regress into Corepack writing under `/.cache/node`. The local kind smoke advanced through dependency startup after Docker build-cache cleanup, then exposed the migration cache bug; a full rerun on this workstation still needs more Docker disk headroom for the kind node before it can produce complete live evidence.

Latest Kubernetes live-smoke disk preflight hardening prevents disposable kind runs from partially deploying when the node is already too full to complete smoke-owned dependency startup. Active `kind-*` contexts now run a Docker-node free-space check after image preparation and before namespace creation; the default `KUBERNETES_LIVE_SMOKE_KIND_MIN_FREE_MIB=8192` can be set to `0` only to disable the local kind guard for an explicitly smaller smoke target. This keeps the developer/staging evidence path fail-fast and metadata-only while preserving the target GA path that uses reviewed registry-hosted digest-pinned images and real cluster evidence.

Latest target-quality evidence hardening removes raw eval subject identifiers from the live evidence artifact without migration churn. `pnpm smoke:quality:target` now records eval agent/workspace references as SHA-256 hashes plus presence booleans, sets explicit top-level raw subject ID redaction flags, and the GA validator rejects legacy target-quality evidence that carries raw `agentId` or `workspaceId` fields or omits subject hashes. `GET /api/v1/admin/target-quality/posture`, OpenAPI, and `client.admin.targetQualityPosture()` expose the new redaction booleans while continuing to omit evidence bodies, target origins, analytics CSV rows, raw agent IDs, raw workspace IDs, replay queries, replay hit IDs, eval inputs/outputs, and secret values. Contract coverage now proves the loopback target can return raw IDs while persisted evidence remains hash-only, and `pnpm smoke:ga:evidence-contract` proves raw-ID artifacts fail the Phase 32 target-quality gate. Product completion still requires collecting live target-quality evidence in the selected target deployment.

Latest GA target env-file hardening closes the operator handoff gap between the generated blank skeleton and confirmed live-gate execution without schema or migration churn. `pnpm ga:target-preflight -- --env-file dist/ci/ga-target.env.private` and `pnpm ga:target-execute-ready -- --env-file dist/ci/ga-target.env.private` now load simple dotenv-style private assignments, skip blank placeholders from the skeleton, avoid shell-sourcing or interpolation, and write only counts, variable names, warning codes, and explicit redaction flags. `romeo.ga-target-execution.v1`, `GET /api/v1/admin/ga/evidence-posture`, and `client.admin.gaEvidencePosture()` now expose sanitized env-file metadata while omitting env-file values, file bodies, command text, and command output. The preflight contract smoke now proves private env-file loading can satisfy target-quality and release-readback environment checks without leaking values, and the execution contract smoke proves a private env-file value reaches child commands without leaking into evidence; the target preflight report remains metadata-only and still does not satisfy any live GA gate.

Latest GA target environment-template hardening adds an operator-prep artifact without schema or migration churn. `pnpm ga:target-env-template` reads reviewed `romeo.ga-target-evidence-plan.v1` and writes redacted `romeo.ga-target-env-template.v1` plus an optional blank `.env` skeleton containing required command names, required environment variable names, any-of variable groups, optional variable names, required file placeholders, evidence target paths, and stable blocker codes grouped by gate. It intentionally omits command text, command output, raw environment values, tokens, evidence bodies, unsafe absolute paths, and raw target-plan check bodies. GitHub CI and `pnpm ci:branch-protection-plan` now require the target env-template contract smoke, and CI generates `dist/ci/ga-target-env-template.json` plus `dist/ci/ga-target.env.example` after the target plan. This improves target environment handoff while preserving the rule that only gate-specific live evidence can satisfy GA.

Latest GA target execution hardening adds the confirmation-gated backend/operator runner and sanitized admin API readback for the remaining live gates without schema or migration churn. `pnpm ga:target-execute-ready` reads reviewed `romeo.ga-target-evidence-plan.v1`, executes only gates with `operatorAction.state=ready_to_run`, skips prerequisite-blocked and redacted-command gates, and writes `romeo.ga-target-execution.v1` with command hashes, exit status, timings, skip reasons, and explicit redaction proof instead of command text or command output. `GET /api/v1/admin/ga/evidence-posture`, `client.admin.gaEvidencePosture()`, Compose, and Helm now accept `GA_TARGET_EXECUTION_PATH` and return sanitized `targetExecution` status, summary, execution counts, gate command hashes, skip/failure reasons, timings, evidence-target status, and warning/redaction flags without returning mounted paths, command text, command output, raw environment values, evidence bodies, or tokens. The command requires `GA_TARGET_EXECUTE_CONFIRM=run-ready-gates` or `--confirm-run-ready-gates` before running target commands; otherwise it emits a non-mutating confirmation-required report. GitHub CI and `pnpm ci:branch-protection-plan` now require the target-execution contract smoke beside the target preflight/plan checks. Validation passed script syntax checks, `pnpm smoke:ga:target-execution-contract -- --output dist/ci/ga-target-execution-contract-smoke.json`, docs command checking, branch-protection plan generation, strict greenfield baseline review, default and opt-in bridge OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, GA checklist generation, target preflight generation, target evidence-plan generation, dry-run target execution evidence generation, target-preflight and target-plan contract smokes, Compose env contract smoke, Helm env-schema contract smoke, Kubernetes render smoke, `@romeo/config`, `@romeo/core`, and `@romeo/api-client` typechecks, config tests, focused core API/readiness tests with 542 passing tests, API-client tests with 108 passing tests, and migration inventory review. Local target execution reports 10 selected gates, 0 ready-to-run gates, 0 commands executed, and 10 skipped prerequisite-blocked gates because this workstation still lacks the target Kubernetes, release, API, and monitoring credentials; the checklist remains 19 of 29 with the same ten target-environment blockers.

Latest GA target-plan and OpenAPI route-coverage hardening improves the backend/admin readiness contract without schema or migration churn. `romeo.ga-target-evidence-plan.v1` and `GET /api/v1/admin/ga/evidence-posture` now expose per-gate `operatorAction` metadata with `ready_to_run`, `blocked_on_prerequisites`, `command_redacted`, and `unknown` states plus sanitized command-availability, prerequisite-blocked, and blocker-code readback. OpenAPI route-coverage evidence now distinguishes public `/api/v1` route registrations from unique public method/path route keys, so the optional OpenWebUI-shaped reference bridge can keep duplicate alias registrations while the GA validator still requires every registration to be covered and OpenAPI operation counts to match the unique route keys. Validation passed script syntax checks, default and opt-in bridge OpenAPI route coverage, GA evidence-contract smoke, GA target-plan contract smoke, `@romeo/core` and `@romeo/api-client` typechecks, focused core API tests with 541 passing tests, API-client tests with 108 passing tests, Python SDK drift, docs command checking, strict greenfield baseline review, GA checklist generation, target preflight generation, and target-plan generation. The GA checklist remains 19 of 29 gates satisfied with the same ten target-environment blockers, and the migration inventory remains only `packages/db/migrations/0000_greenfield_baseline.sql`.

Latest production-readiness hardening wires mounted GA evidence posture into `/api/v1/admin/readiness` without schema or migration churn. The new `ga_evidence` readiness check reuses the sanitized GA posture reader for `GA_CHECKLIST_PATH`, `GA_TARGET_PREFLIGHT_PATH`, `GA_TARGET_PLAN_PATH`, `GA_TARGET_EXECUTION_PATH`, and `GA_EVIDENCE_BUNDLE_PATH`; missing mounts remain informational for local/self-hosted installs, while blocked, failed, or invalid mounted GA evidence becomes a critical readiness finding with only configured booleans, status values, blocker counts, live-readiness counts, execution counts, and warning codes. OpenAPI now documents the typed `ReadinessReport` and `ReadinessCheck` schema for `/admin/readiness` instead of returning a generic object envelope. Regression coverage proves blocked checklist evidence fails readiness without leaking mounted paths, raw evidence paths, raw failure strings, or exception owner text. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` and `@romeo/api-client` typechecks, the focused core API/readiness target with 542 passing tests, default and opt-in bridge OpenAPI route coverage, and Python SDK drift. No migration files were added.

Latest enterprise identity OpenAPI hardening replaces generic response envelopes for the SSO/auth-provider admin surface without runtime or migration churn. `GET/PATCH /api/v1/admin/sso-settings`, `POST /api/v1/admin/sso-settings/test`, `POST /api/v1/admin/sso/oidc/deprovision`, `GET /api/v1/admin/auth-providers/catalog`, `GET/PATCH /api/v1/admin/auth-providers/settings`, and `POST /api/v1/admin/auth-providers/settings/test` now point at typed OpenAPI schemas for sanitized SSO settings, provider presets, connection-test reports, deprovisioning results, provider catalog entries, effective global/org provider settings, per-protocol connection summaries, and auth-provider connection-test reports. The new schemas live in a dedicated identity-auth OpenAPI component module, and the SSO update/deprovision request schemas are typed as well. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` and `@romeo/api-client` typechecks, focused core API/readiness tests with 542 passing tests, API-client tests with 108 passing tests, default and opt-in bridge OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, and exported OpenAPI inspection proving each targeted identity endpoint references the typed schema. No migration files were added.

Latest backend auth/session OpenAPI hardening replaces generic envelopes for the local auth, local MFA, user-session, and support-impersonation surfaces without runtime or migration churn. `POST /api/v1/auth/local/login`, `POST /api/v1/auth/local/mfa/verify`, `GET|POST /api/v1/auth/local/status|password`, TOTP enrollment/confirmation/recovery-code generation/disable, `GET|POST /api/v1/sessions`, session revoke routes, and support impersonation session/request routes now reference typed schemas for sanitized sessions, created-session tokens, authenticated-vs-MFA-required login results, local MFA factor/status/recovery-code payloads, TOTP enrollment secrets, support session reports, and support approval requests. The response schemas live in a dedicated session-auth OpenAPI component module, while `CreateSessionRequest` and `CreateSupportSessionRequest` are tightened in place to match the route validators. No migration files were added.

Latest bootstrap/profile OpenAPI hardening completes the previously frontend-gating profile-read/update contract without runtime or migration churn. `GET /api/v1/me` already returns `subject.email`, `subject.name`, the optional current `user`, and server-authoritative `deployment.tenancyMode`; `PATCH /api/v1/me` already updates the current user's email/name, blocks support-session updates, keeps local-login email credentials synchronized, and audits only changed-field booleans. OpenAPI now documents `PATCH /api/v1/me` as returning `User`, `GET /api/v1/organizations` and `GET /api/v1/workspaces` as typed arrays, `POST /api/v1/workspaces` as `Workspace`, and `/health` as `HealthStatus` instead of generic object envelopes. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` and `@romeo/api-client` typechecks, focused core API/readiness tests with 542 passing tests, API-client tests with 108 passing tests, default and opt-in bridge OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, docs command checking, strict greenfield baseline review, GA checklist generation, and exported OpenAPI inspection proving the changed bootstrap endpoints reference the named schemas. No migration files were added.

Latest admin user lifecycle OpenAPI hardening replaces generic response envelopes for the backend role-promotion and SSO-fallback account-management surface without runtime or migration churn. `GET /api/v1/users`, `POST /api/v1/users/{userId}/disable`, and `PATCH /api/v1/users/{userId}/role` now return typed `User` data, while `POST /api/v1/users/{userId}/local-password` returns typed `LocalAuthStatus` for admin-managed local password fallback. The implementation already supports local and SSO-provisioned user promotion/demotion with confirmation, self-role-change denial, global-admin elevation checks, credential revocation on role changes, admin local password reset with local-auth status readback, and metadata-only audit records. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` and `@romeo/api-client` typechecks, focused core API/readiness tests with 542 passing tests, API-client tests with 108 passing tests, default and opt-in bridge OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, docs command checking, strict greenfield baseline review, GA checklist generation, and exported OpenAPI inspection proving the admin user endpoints reference the named schemas. No migration files were added.

Latest workflow transaction-boundary hardening closes the remaining durable-core retry linked-run atomicity gap without schema or migration churn. Workflow agent runs can now be prepared inside the caller's repository transaction through deferred run starts; chat/message/run/usage/retrieval-event records for workflow-spawned model runs commit with workflow state and audit writes, while provider execution starts only after the transaction succeeds. This covers workflow start, approval continuation, resume/recover continuation, retry, and browser-task completion paths, including outer browser worker transactions. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck, the full core Vitest suite with 447 passing tests, and focused Prettier. No migration files were added.

Latest support impersonation transaction hardening closes the durable-core support approval notification gap without schema or migration churn. Direct support-session creation, approval-request creation, approval, rejection, and revocation now write session/request state, sanitized audit rows, and user notification ledger rows inside one repository transaction; notification enqueue failure rolls the whole support action back. Notification contracts now include support impersonation request/session event types and resource scopes while preserving the existing chat mention path. Focused validation passed core, db, and API-client typechecks, the core Vitest suite with 450 passing tests, and db notification repository/conformance tests with 111 passing tests and 2 skipped environment cases. No migration files were added.

Latest run-start transaction hardening closes the durable-core normal API run-start atomicity gap without schema or migration churn. Public run starts now prepare read-only provider/knowledge/tool context and object-store attachment uploads first, then commit quota updates, user message rows, attachment-part rows, run rows, retrieval events, and started usage records in one repository transaction. Provider execution starts only after that transaction commits, and uploaded attachment objects are best-effort deleted if the commit fails. Failure-injection now proves a `run.started` usage write failure rolls back the user message, run row, retrieval event, and usage ledger. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 451 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest knowledge import transaction hardening closes the inline source import and uploaded source completion portions of the durable-core knowledge transaction gap without schema or migration churn. Inline knowledge-source registration now commits source rows, quota/usage ledgers, chunk replacement/creation, source indexed/failed state, and registered-usage records in one repository transaction, with best-effort object-store cleanup if the transaction fails after content persistence. Uploaded source completion now reads the object first, then commits chunk replacement/creation, source state, and completed-usage records in one repository transaction. Failure-injection now proves `storage.source_registered` and `storage.source_completed` usage write failures roll back source/chunk/usage state, and inline failures also remove the stored object. Embedding indexing still keeps provider and external vector-store calls outside the database transaction and remains an evidence/review item rather than broad in-transaction provider work. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 453 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest knowledge upload/extraction/reindex transaction hardening closes the remaining local knowledge-source persistence gap without schema or migration churn. Presigned upload registration now commits the pending source row, quota reservation, upload usage event, and presign metadata handoff in one repository transaction. Deferred PDF/Office extraction now commits chunk replacement/creation, source indexed state, extracted-usage event, and background-job completion in one transaction after object bytes and text extraction are complete. Text source reindexing now snapshots prior source-object bytes, writes replacement content, commits chunk replacement/creation, source indexed state, reindexed-usage event, and background-job completion in one transaction, then sends external-vector tombstones only after that transaction succeeds; if the transaction fails, it restores the previous object bytes before failing the reindex job and does not tombstone external vectors. Failure-injection proves `storage.source_registered` upload-registration failures roll back the pending source row and usage state, `storage.source_extracted` deferred-extraction failures leave no indexed chunks or extracted-usage row while failing the extraction job for operator retry/review, and `storage.source_reindexed` failures preserve the previous chunks/source hash plus source-object bytes while failing the reindex job and skipping external-vector deletion. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest transaction-boundary suite with 545 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest knowledge-base, comment, and prompt-template transaction hardening closes local backend consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Knowledge-base creation now commits the KB row, owner grants, service-account owner resolution where needed, and audit row inside one repository transaction; knowledge-base update now commits metadata and audit together. Chat-comment creation now commits the comment row, mention notification rows, notification delivery ledger rows, and audit row inside one transaction. Prompt-template create/update/share/delete now commit template rows, owner/share grants, and audit rows together. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back knowledge-base create/update and prompt-template mutation state, while mention-notification failures roll back comment and notification state. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 458 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest device authorization transaction hardening closes a native-client security consistency gap without schema or migration churn. Device authorization create now commits the API key, device authorization row, refresh-token hash, and audit row together. Device authorization refresh now re-reads the refresh-token hash inside the transaction, rotates the refresh-token hash, creates the replacement API key, revokes the previous API key, and audits the refresh atomically. Device authorization revoke now commits authorization revocation, active API-key revocation, and audit together. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back create, refresh, and revoke state, including preserving the previous refresh-token hash and active API key on failed refresh. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and focused core Vitest coverage with 461 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest voice-profile transaction hardening closes local voice catalog/profile consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Manual voice-profile creation now commits the profile row, use grant, and `voice.profile.create` audit row together. Voice catalog sync still reads the provider catalog outside the database transaction, then commits missing profile imports, use grants, and the `voice.catalog_sync` audit row together. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back manual profile creation and catalog-sync imports, including associated use grants. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 463 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest billing transaction hardening closes local entitlement consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Billing plan application now commits the plan, quota-template bucket create/update operations, and `billing.plan_applied` audit row together. External billing sync now commits the derived plan/quota changes, plan-applied audit, and external-event audit in one transaction. Entitlement reconciliation now commits quota repair and reconciliation audit together, and lifecycle enforcement now commits plan status changes and lifecycle audit together. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back plan/quota application, external sync, reconciliation repair, and lifecycle status transitions. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 467 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest file-storage transaction hardening closes local native-file consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Inline file creation now writes the object first, then commits storage quota usage, file metadata, owner grants, and audit in one repository transaction, with best-effort object cleanup if the DB commit fails. Direct and resumable upload-session creation now commit quota usage, file metadata, owner grants, and audit together. Direct upload completion now commits file availability and audit together, and file deletion now commits deleted metadata and audit before removing stored objects so an audit failure cannot remove content while metadata remains active. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back inline create with object cleanup, direct session create, direct completion, and delete state. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 471 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest local-auth transaction hardening closes local credential/MFA consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Self-service and admin password set now commit password credential create/update, admin session revocation where applicable, and audit together. TOTP enrollment, confirmation, disable, recovery-code generation, and recovery-code consumption now commit factor state and audit together. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back password credential creation and TOTP enrollment state. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 473 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest user-lifecycle transaction hardening closes local role/admin lifecycle consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. User disable now commits disabled state, user API-key/session/support-session revocation, and audit together. User role update now commits the role change, credential/session revocation, and audit together, preserving the global-admin guard for global role changes. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back disabled state, role changes, and credential/session revocation. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 475 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest API-key transaction hardening closes a high-risk credential lifecycle consistency gap without schema or migration churn. User-owned API key creation now commits the key row and `api_key.create` audit row together; service-account API key creation re-checks service-account disabled/scope state inside the transaction before committing the key row and audit; API key revocation now commits the revoked timestamp and `api_key.revoke` audit row together. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back user key creation, service-account key creation, and key revocation. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 478 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest service-account transaction hardening closes another credential lifecycle consistency gap without schema or migration churn. Service-account creation now commits the account row and `service_account.create` audit row together. Service-account disable now commits the disabled timestamp, active service-account API-key revocations, and `service_account.disable` audit row together, so a failed audit cannot leave a disabled principal or revoked key without durable evidence. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back service-account creation, disable state, and active key revocation. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 480 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest group/RBAC transaction hardening closes access-control consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Group creation now commits the group row and `group.create` audit row together. Group member add/remove now commit membership changes and their audit rows together, so RBAC changes cannot persist without durable audit evidence. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back group creation, member addition, and member removal. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 483 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest provider/workspace transaction hardening closes admin control-plane consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Provider creation, model pricing updates, provider model-sync persistence, workspace creation, and workspace archive now commit durable rows and audit rows together. Provider model discovery remains outside the database transaction, then model upserts and audit commit atomically. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back provider creation, model pricing changes, provider model sync inserts, workspace creation, and workspace archive state. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 488 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest managed-secret and webhook transaction hardening closes secret/control-plane audit gaps without schema or migration churn. Local managed-secret creation now commits the encrypted system-setting row and `admin.managed_secret.create` audit row together. Webhook subscription creation now emits `webhook.create` audit evidence and commits it with the subscription row; single and bulk disable commit subscription state and audit rows together. External vault writes and webhook HTTP delivery attempts remain outside database transactions because they are external side effects. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back local secret creation, webhook creation, single disable, and bulk disable state. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 492 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest tool connector transaction hardening closes admin integration consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Tool connector auth updates, connector enablement, network-policy updates, and operation enablement now commit durable state and sanitized audit rows together. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back auth config, connector enabled state, network policy, and operation enabled state. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 496 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest SSO settings transaction hardening closes another identity control-plane consistency gap without schema or migration churn. OIDC SSO settings updates now commit the settings row and sanitized `admin.sso_settings.update` audit row together. Failure-injection proves audit failure rolls back OIDC settings persistence. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 497 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest tenant lifecycle transaction hardening closes multi-tenant control-plane consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Organization update, suspension, reactivation, deletion request, deletion-request cancellation, and deletion-finalization evidence recording now commit tenant state, abuse-control or deletion-control settings, and sanitized audit rows together. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back organization metadata, suspension state, deletion-request state, cancellation state, and deletion evidence. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 503 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest directory-sync and SCIM transaction hardening closes enterprise identity lifecycle consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Directory-sync apply now commits user disables, credential revocation, stale group-membership removal, and `directory_sync.apply` audit together. SCIM user create/replace/patch/deactivate and group create/replace/patch now commit user/group/membership/credential state and SCIM audit rows together, while SCIM group delete already used a transactional cleanup boundary. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back directory-sync user disable, API-key revocation, membership removal, SCIM user creation, SCIM user deactivation/credential revocation, and SCIM group creation/membership writes. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 507 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest eval and collaboration transaction hardening closes API consistency gaps for evaluation lifecycle records and user workspace organization features without schema or migration churn. Eval suite/case creation, result rating, and run/result completion now commit durable rows and audit evidence together, with provider execution kept outside the database transaction. Collaboration resource favorites, shares, folder create/update/delete, and folder item add/delete now use transaction-scoped audit writes, including owner-grant creation for new folders and resource-grant creation for shares. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back eval suite creation, eval result rating, collaboration share grants, and folder owner-grant creation. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 511 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest native channel transaction hardening closes collaboration-channel consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Native channel create, update, delete, member add, and member remove now commit channel/member rows and sanitized audit rows together; channel creation includes initial membership, updates include newly requested members, and deletion includes member cleanup inside the same transaction. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back channel creation, update name/member additions, channel deletion/member cleanup, explicit member additions, and explicit member removals. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 516 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest SSO deprovision transaction hardening closes an identity lifecycle audit gap without schema or migration churn. OIDC deprovision now commits user disablement, credential/session/support-session revocation, nested `user.disable` audit, and `admin.sso_oidc.deprovision` audit together through a transaction-scoped user lifecycle service. Failure-injection proves a deprovision audit failure rolls back the disabled timestamp and nested lifecycle audit. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 517 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest agent capability-binding audit hardening closes silent control-plane changes without schema or migration churn. Agent tool-binding updates now commit enablement/approval state and `agent.tool_binding.update` audit together, and agent knowledge-base binding updates now commit enablement state and `agent.knowledge_binding.update` audit together. Audit metadata is limited to agent/tool/knowledge identifiers plus booleans and does not include prompts, knowledge content, tool inputs, connector payloads, or secrets. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back tool approval-state changes and knowledge-base enablement changes. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 519 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest local session transaction hardening closes credential lifecycle consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Standard local session creation now commits the session row and `session.create` audit row together. Current-session revocation, targeted revocation, and revoke-other-session batches now commit revoked timestamps and `session.revoke` audit rows together, so audit failure cannot leave a new active session or revoke credentials without durable evidence. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back session creation, current-session revocation, targeted revocation, and multi-session revoke-other batches. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 523 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest delegated OAuth transaction hardening closes connected-account revocation consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Delegated OAuth revoke now performs provider revocation as an external side effect, then commits local revoked status and sanitized `delegated_oauth.revoke` audit together in a repository transaction. Failure-injection proves a revoke audit failure rolls back local active connection state and revoked timestamps. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 524 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest quota admin transaction hardening closes silent entitlement-control changes without schema or migration churn. Direct quota create/update/delete now commit quota bucket state and sanitized `quota.create`, `quota.update`, or `quota.delete` audit rows together, while external quota coordinator sync/delete remains after the database transaction. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back quota creation, limit/reset updates, and deletion. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest suite with 527 passing tests; an existing quota-exhaustion test now asserts blocked tool execution emits no `tool.execute` audit while preserving the new quota admin audit. No migration files were added.

Latest data connector creation transaction hardening closes a connector control-plane audit gap without schema or migration churn. Data connector creation now commits service-account actor lookup/creation, connector state, and sanitized `data_connector.create` audit rows in one repository transaction. Connector runtime readiness checks, external fetches, object storage, and knowledge ingestion remain outside that database transaction boundary. Failure-injection proves an audit failure rolls back connector creation and leaves no `data_connector.create` audit. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest transaction-boundary suite with 528 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest audited chat lifecycle transaction hardening closes history control-plane consistency gaps without schema or migration churn. Chat title updates, archive, unarchive, legal-hold set, and legal-hold clear now commit the chat row and sanitized audit row together. Failure-injection proves each audited chat mutation rolls back state when the matching `chat.update`, `chat.archive`, `chat.unarchive`, `chat.legal_hold.update`, or `chat.legal_hold.clear` audit write fails. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest transaction-boundary suite with 533 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest knowledge-source deletion transaction hardening closes a RAG data-retention audit gap without schema or migration churn. Local source deletion now commits chunk/embedding cleanup, source removal, `storage.source_deleted` usage, and sanitized `knowledge.source.delete` audit rows together after external vector/object-store deletion has completed. Failure-injection proves an audit failure rolls back the source row, chunks, embeddings, and usage event. External vector-store and object-store deletion remain outside the database transaction boundary and still require target evidence review. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest transaction-boundary suite with 534 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest secret-rotation transaction hardening closes an MFA and managed-secret envelope audit gap without schema or migration churn. `POST /api/v1/admin/secret-rotation/rewrap` now rewrites local MFA envelopes, rewrites local `romeo-secret://` managed-secret envelopes, and writes sanitized `admin.secret_rotation.rewrap` audit evidence inside one repository transaction; preview remains read-only. Failure-injection proves an audit failure rolls back both envelope classes so old-key decryption still works and new-key decryption does not. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest transaction-boundary suite with 535 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest governance retention transaction hardening closes retention-control audit gaps without schema or migration churn. Retention policy updates now commit the policy row and `governance.retention.update` audit together. Retention enforcement now commits audit-log deletion and `governance.retention.enforce` audit together after external artifact cleanup has completed, so an audit failure cannot silently delete audit rows. Failure-injection proves audit failures roll back policy updates and old audit-log deletion. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest transaction-boundary suite with 537 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest data-export package transaction hardening closes governed export package registry audit gaps without schema or migration churn. Package creation now writes the object-store artifact first, then commits registry metadata and `governance.data_export.package.create` audit together, with best-effort artifact cleanup if that commit fails. Package deletion now validates object presence first, commits registry removal and `governance.data_export.package.delete` audit together, then deletes the object after commit. Failure-injection proves create audit failures leave no package registry entry and remove the artifact, while delete audit failures preserve the registry entry and object. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest transaction-boundary suite with 539 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest voice artifact transaction hardening closes explicit voice artifact deletion audit gaps without schema or migration churn. Voice artifact deletion now commits usage-event metadata redaction and `voice.artifact.delete` audit evidence in one repository transaction, then removes the object-store artifact only after that commit succeeds. Failure-injection proves audit failures preserve the active usage metadata and stored artifact. Focused validation passed `@romeo/core` typecheck and the core Vitest transaction-boundary suite with 540 passing tests. No migration files were added.

Latest backend posture-contract hardening aligns the remaining evidence-backed enterprise/resilience admin posture APIs with their GA validators without schema or migration churn. Provider outage, failed-migration drill, network-partition drill, secret-rotation drill, identity-live, and analytics-authz posture services now emit exact GA failure codes for invalid deployment metadata, missing required checks, live-mode/status gaps, validator-specific drill/readback gaps, redaction gaps, and evidence failure-code presence while suppressing arbitrary raw evidence failure strings. `GET /api/v1/admin/ga/evidence-posture` now collapses mounted checklist evidence and exception `failures` arrays to Romeo-owned presence codes, and `GET /api/v1/admin/postgres/operational-posture` now treats slow-query, lock, and archival evidence `failures` arrays as attention-required posture with GA-aligned presence codes instead of relaying raw telemetry or decision failure strings. OpenAPI and the TypeScript SDK warning contracts now include the new deployment/failure-code/live-readback/Postgres failure-presence warning buckets, and focused strictness tests are split by backend domain in `target-resilience-posture-strictness.test.ts` and `enterprise-live-posture-strictness.test.ts` with additional API regressions for GA and Postgres posture redaction. Validation passed core and API-client typechecks, the core Vitest suite with 446 passing tests, GA evidence-contract smoke, OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, docs command checking, strict greenfield baseline review, GA checklist generation, focused Prettier, and a posture-service scan confirming only bounded failure-presence checks remain. The GA checklist remains 19 of 29 with the same ten target-environment blockers. No migration files were added.

Latest tenant purge posture hardening aligns deletion-control admin readback with the optional Phase 33 tenant-purge GA validator without schema or migration churn. `GET /api/v1/admin/tenant-deletion/purge-evidence-posture` now fails mounted `romeo.tenant-purge-evidence.v1` with GA-aligned failure codes for non-passed status, non-live mode, invalid deployment metadata, missing required checks, evidence failure-code presence, missing app database/object-store purge counts, missing external vector/backup/operational-log/support-bundle/external-secret reviews, missing storage-class counts, missing retention-day proof, and redaction leaks. The posture response now emits typed admin warnings for deployment, storage-review, retention-day, and evidence-failure-code gaps while suppressing arbitrary raw evidence failure strings; it still returns only evidence status, mode/deployment, required-check coverage, tenant purge counts, storage-class counts, retention-day metadata, warning/failure codes, and redaction booleans. It continues to omit mounted paths, evidence bodies, object-store keys, vector values, backup locations, operational log bodies, support bundle bodies, tokens, and secret values. Focused validation passed core and API-client typechecks, focused tenant purge posture API tests, GA evidence-contract smoke, OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, and focused Prettier. No migration files were added.

Latest alert-firing posture hardening aligns Prometheus/Alertmanager admin readback with the Phase 34 live-alert GA validator without schema or migration churn. `GET /api/v1/admin/alert-firing/posture` now emits alert-specific missing-check failure codes, exposes sanitized `requiredFiringMissingCount`, and keeps missing required-alert names out of failure codes and responses while still requiring every configured required alert to appear in Prometheus firing readback before reporting ready. The posture response still returns only evidence status, live/dry-run mode, required-check coverage, provider/queue/backup category counts, Prometheus/Alertmanager counts, warning/failure codes, and redaction booleans while omitting mounted paths, evidence bodies, Prometheus or Alertmanager URLs, bearer tokens, alert names, labels, annotations, raw responses, raw alert payloads, tokens, and secrets. OpenAPI and the TypeScript SDK include the sanitized missing-firing count. Focused validation passed core and API-client typechecks, focused alert posture API tests, GA evidence-contract smoke, OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, and focused Prettier. No migration files were added.

Latest Kubernetes posture maintainability work splits the strict mounted-evidence readback path into smaller backend modules without changing the admin API shape, OpenAPI contract, or database schema. `kubernetes-posture-service.ts` now focuses on authorization, mounted file readback, evidence summarization, warning aggregation, and sanitized report assembly; `kubernetes-posture-definitions.ts` owns evidence definitions and response types; and `kubernetes-posture-validation.ts` owns GA-aligned kind-specific failure-code validation for live smoke, workers, NetworkPolicy, DR, tiered-RAG, load/soak, and KEDA. Focused validation passed core typecheck, Kubernetes posture API tests, GA evidence-contract smoke, and OpenAPI route coverage. No migration files were added.

Latest Kubernetes posture API hardening makes mounted admin readback enforce the same metadata contract shape that the GA validators expect for target-cluster evidence, without schema or migration churn. `GET /api/v1/admin/kubernetes/posture` now applies stricter required-check lists and kind-specific failure codes for live-smoke image/readback/log-redaction posture, worker required names/crash recovery/log-redaction posture, NetworkPolicy enforcement/selected-component/log-redaction posture, CloudNativePG and external-Postgres DR namespace isolation/connection/evidence/readback/log-redaction posture, tiered-RAG vector posture, load/soak tier/duration/per-run-summary/raw-image-ID/log-redaction posture, and optional KEDA target/retry/job/log-redaction posture. Mounted evidence with `status=passed` can no longer appear ready in the admin API when it would fail `pnpm ga:checklist` for stale or incomplete metadata. The endpoint still returns only sanitized booleans, counts, warning/failure codes, aggregate metrics, and redaction flags; it omits evidence paths, evidence bodies, Kubernetes object bodies, pod logs, namespace values, image refs, database URLs, and secrets. Focused validation passed `pnpm --filter @romeo/core check`, `pnpm --filter @romeo/core test -- src/api.test.ts -t "Kubernetes"`, OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, and GA evidence-contract smoke. No migration files were added; migration inventory remains `packages/db/migrations/0000_greenfield_baseline.sql`.

Latest Kubernetes load/soak evidence/posture hardening aligns the live collector, GA validator, and `GET /api/v1/admin/kubernetes/posture` with the same Phase 34 contract without schema or migration churn. `pnpm smoke:kubernetes:load-soak` now records namespace readback, fixture validation, metadata-only live scale-load summaries, non-local tier, observed soak, pod inventory, and pod-log redaction checks, and it no longer writes raw pod `imageID` values into evidence. `pnpm ga:checklist` now rejects load/soak evidence that omits namespace readback, fixture validation, per-run live summaries with latency counts, raw-image-ID redaction, repeated-load, soak, pod-inventory, or pod-log-redaction proof. The Kubernetes posture service now requires those same check names, so mounted valid load/soak evidence is not falsely failed by stale posture requirements. Validation passed syntax checks, dry-run Kubernetes load/soak evidence generation, strengthened GA evidence-contract smoke, core typecheck, OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, docs command checking, strict greenfield baseline review, and `pnpm ga:checklist -- --output dist/ci/ga-checklist-load-soak-summary-redaction-final.json`, which remains blocked at 19 of 29 gates satisfied because the ten remaining blockers require target-environment evidence. No migration files were added; migration inventory remains `packages/db/migrations/0000_greenfield_baseline.sql`.

Latest Kubernetes tiered-RAG evidence hardening aligns target-cluster evidence with the active vector-plan API contract without schema or migration churn. `pnpm smoke:kubernetes:tiered-rag` now fails before writing passed live evidence unless the tiered query plan reports valid vector driver, physical isolation mode, external-vector routing posture, namespace policy, partitioning policy, and per-entry vector scopes matching every authorized user-private, workspace, org, and shared corpus entry. The generated `romeo.kubernetes-tiered-rag-smoke.v1` evidence records only metadata-safe driver/policy enums, booleans, plan-entry count, and vector-scope driver counts, and `pnpm smoke:ga:evidence-contract` now rejects Kubernetes tiered-RAG evidence that omits `tiered_rag_vector_plan_posture_reported`, has invalid namespace/partitioning policy, or has scope counts that do not match the active vector driver. `GET /api/v1/admin/kubernetes/posture`, OpenAPI, and `client.admin.kubernetesPosture()` now expose the sanitized mounted vector posture summary for tiered-RAG evidence while still omitting raw namespace values, evidence bodies, pod logs, vector values, endpoints, and secrets. Validation passed syntax checks, core and API-client typechecks, API-client tests, strengthened GA evidence-contract smoke, OpenAPI route coverage with and without the optional OpenWebUI bridge, Python SDK drift, focused Prettier, docs command checking, and migration inventory review. No migration files were added; migration inventory remains `packages/db/migrations/0000_greenfield_baseline.sql`.

Latest tiered RAG vector-plan API hardening keeps the query contract aligned with the active vector deployment without schema or migration churn. `POST /api/v1/knowledge-bases/query` now reports the active per-entry `vectorScope.driver` as `pgvector` or `qdrant` instead of hardcoding `pgvector`, carries the active physical isolation mode into every authorized plan entry, and exposes metadata-only `partitioningConfigured` / `partitioningPolicy` beside `namespaceConfigured` / `namespacePolicy` in `plan.posture`. The TypeScript API client and OpenAPI schema now accept Qdrant vector scopes and partitioning posture, and `pnpm smoke:rag-vector:enterprise-contract` now fails if a Qdrant tiered query omits the plan-level namespace/partitioning policy or entry-level Qdrant vector scope. Validation passed core typecheck, focused knowledge retrieval plan tests, knowledge API tests, API client typecheck/tests, OpenAPI route coverage, Python SDK drift, strengthened RAG/vector enterprise contract smoke, focused Prettier, and migration inventory review. Latest GA target-preflight command hardening aligns live evidence commands with the checked `ROMEO_API_KEY` prerequisite without schema or migration churn. `pnpm ga:target-preflight` no longer emits unchecked `$SCOPED_ADMIN_OR_OPERATOR_KEY` command prefixes for optional KEDA, Kubernetes workers, target-quality capture, Kubernetes tiered-RAG, live edge enforcement, or Kubernetes load/soak; it now passes `--api-key $ROMEO_API_KEY` explicitly where the preflight checks `env:ROMEO_API_KEY`. The GA and Kubernetes operator docs now use the same contract. Validation passed focused Prettier, `pnpm smoke:ga:target-preflight-contract -- --output dist/ci/ga-target-preflight-contract-api-key-command-final.json`, `pnpm docs:check-commands -- --output dist/ci/docs-command-check-api-key-command-final.json`, `pnpm ga:target-preflight -- --checklist dist/ci/ga-checklist-current-continuation.json --output dist/ci/ga-target-preflight-api-key-command-final.json`, `pnpm ga:target-plan -- --preflight dist/ci/ga-target-preflight-api-key-command-final.json --output dist/ci/ga-target-plan-api-key-command-final.json`, `pnpm ga:checklist -- --output dist/ci/ga-checklist-api-key-command-final.json`, and `pnpm review:baseline -- --strict --output dist/ci/greenfield-baseline-api-key-command.json`. Latest Qdrant DR target-preflight hardening makes the optional external-vector restored-stack gate produce runnable operator commands instead of placeholder text, without schema or migration churn. `pnpm ga:target-preflight` now emits the three required `pnpm smoke:qdrant:dr` calls for prepare-source, verify-restore, and cleanup-source with explicit `--api-key`, `--run-secret`, `--namespace-policy`, and `--partitioning-policy` arguments; `pnpm ga:target-plan` preserves those commands as safe command text because they contain only env placeholders. The preflight now treats `QDRANT_DR_RUN_SECRET`, `VECTOR_NAMESPACE_POLICY`, and `VECTOR_PARTITIONING_POLICY` as required for the Qdrant DR command path and blocks missing partitioning policy before operators reach a broken shell expansion. The Qdrant live command likewise uses `--api-key $QDRANT_API_KEY` instead of an env-prefix API-key handoff. Latest audit-integrity GA hardening adds an optional strict Phase 33 gate for regulated deployments without schema or migration churn. `pnpm ga:checklist -- --require-audit-integrity-live` / `GA_REQUIRE_AUDIT_INTEGRITY_LIVE=true` now requires passed live metadata-only `romeo.audit-integrity-evidence.v1`, validates required checks, SIEM/object-store delivery readback, WORM or retention-lock posture, retention policy, time-sync review, checksum-chain proof, failure-code absence, and redaction flags, and marks the gate security-critical and environment-required. `pnpm ga:target-preflight` maps the gate to `AUDIT_INTEGRITY_EVIDENCE_REVIEWED=true pnpm evidence:audit-integrity -- --output dist/ci/audit-integrity-evidence.json` and requires reviewed-evidence confirmation before reporting ready. `AUDIT_INTEGRITY_EVIDENCE_PATH`, `GET /api/v1/admin/audit-integrity/posture`, and `client.admin.auditIntegrityPosture()` still expose sanitized mounted-evidence readback only; they omit mounted paths, evidence bodies, SIEM destinations, object-store destinations, raw audit metadata, actor identifiers, raw SIEM payloads, and secret values.
